The expansion map's total of 17 files is right but its split is wrong in kind (the read's flagged claim F1): the map lists “11 student + 6 teacher”, counting the 5 IPN curriculum PDFs among the student books; the true split is 6 pupil textbooks + 6 teacher guides + 5 curriculum documents. **No *Super Efficace* pupil book is in hand at any primary grade** — the primary pupil books are print-only commercial (EDIG) and were never secured, so Gabon's primary coverage is the IPN curriculum plus the teacher guide for each year; each guide teaches every lesson of the absent pupil book and cites its pages, and the guide rows below cite both the guide page and the pupil-book page the guide gives. Link-only CIAM volumes (3e, Terminale SM/SE and their guides) are not in hand and are cited nowhere. ## Ireland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Ireland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **9 pupil textbooks of the Text & Tests series (The Celtic Press / CJ Fallon): one English volume, Text & Tests 4 (New Edition), and eight Irish-language (Téacs & Trialacha) volumes** — and placed 570 items. Ireland has no government-published textbooks: every book in the set is a commercial in-use title held as a complete free copy. The English TT4 was read in phase3-staging (byte- and SHA-256-verified in place); the eight Irish volumes were re-grabbed from their recorded COGG (cogg.ie) source URLs, each landing byte-exact at its recorded size, every file's identity proved from its cover, copyright and contents pages. The expansion map's count is right: **9 student + 0 teacher** — the one further Ireland file in hand, *Dúchas 2 — Leabhar Scileanna*, is a Junior Cycle Irish-language (Gaeilge) skills book, not Maths, and is cited nowhere in this course. Extent, honestly: five whole-course books (TT1, TT2 GL, TT3, English TT4, TT6); three strand volumes complete as published but covering one strand only (TT4 Irish = Strand 2; TT5 Irish = Strand 1; TT7 Irish = Strand 5); and one supplement booklet (the TT5 Forlíonadh — deferred Strand 1 material and the Central Limit Theorem), complete as published, not a standalone textbook. Two record flags ride with the set (the read's flagged claims F1–F2, both about the library records, not the course): **F1** — 8 of the 9 PDFs are absent from their phase3-staging folders, which hold only books.json + CURRICULUM.md; all eight are secured in GitHub and were re-grabbed byte-exact from COGG for the read, and restoring them into staging is owed on the library side. **F2** — the held TT3 is the updated edition (2024) of the 2013 book, with an added Chapter 19, Inferential Statistics, that no record mentions; its three items are stocked from that chapter and TT3's book entry names the edition wherever it appears. The older strand books and the English TT4 New Edition teach much of the same Leaving Certificate Higher Level ground in two languages and editions (e.g. TT6 chs. 1–2 ≈ TT4 Eng chs. 1–2; TT7 ch. 2 ≈ TT4 Eng ch. 7): each book's row is stocked as its own citation — they are distinct held books, not duplicates — while Ireland itself is counted once in this lesson's taught-in figure. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching section of each book. The placements' named exclusions — every book's chapter tests (“Cuir triail ort féin”), tasks, learning-experience and review sections, chapter summaries and Answers sections, and every book's prefaces, keyword lists and contents pages — are stocked nowhere. Ireland COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Ireland book is a pupil textbook. ## Fiji added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Fiji's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 pupil textbooks of the Ministry of Education / Curriculum Development Unit series, one for each of Years 1–13** — and placed 1,012 items. The expansion map's count is exact: staging `books.json` holds exactly 13 Mathematics entries (one per year, all `kind: student`) and `phase3-push-state.json` lists all 13 as pushed; no Maths teacher guide is in hand (the AQEP Maths Guides for Lower Primary exist only as AnyFlip reader copies in the download list — not in hand, not counted). The staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, so each book was re-fetched for the read — from the repo tree (Contents API) where the pusher had stored it as a blob, else from the book's recorded `source_url` (a Wayback Machine capture of the ministry file, Beacon Media, or Google Drive) — and the grabbed copies were deleted after their placements were written. The read's nine flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) `textbooks.tsv` carries only 9 of the 13 rows (Years 1, 2, 3, 4, 7, 10, 11, 12, 13) — Years 5, 6, 8 and 9 are staged and pushed but missing from the TSV; (2) the Year 2 scan duplicates printed p. 57 and printed p. 58 is absent, so the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand to read and is cited nowhere; (3) the Year 5 capture has no cover/title leaves — the file opens at the Strand 1 divider and the body runs complete, Strand 1 p. 1 to Strand 5 p. 98, its identity resting on the staged record and the body's own structure; (4) Year 4's folio sequence is irregular — folios 44–48 and 54 never appear and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice — but the leaf count closes exactly (85 printed numbers − 6 absent + 2 duplicated = 81 body leaves), every contents strand and unit is present and no activity breaks mid-stream at the gaps, so the evidence points to the printed book's own folio misprints rather than missing leaves, a judgement the file alone cannot prove; (5) Year 3's scan order is scrambled at the end (contents folio ii and printed pp. 46, 51, 52 sit at PDF pp. 76–80) and one activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51 — counted once), with folio tracking accounting for all of printed pp. 1–75; (6) Year 10 exists in two packagings — the staged Beacon Media file (9,099,595 bytes, 202 pp.) is the copy cited; an ECopies capture of the same book is 9,079,205 bytes / 200 pp.; (7) Year 12's first Wayback transfer arrived truncated (11,794,148 bytes, broken cross-reference table) and was discarded — a fresh download delivered the complete book (17,085,310 bytes, 284 pp.), which is what was read; (8) Year 1's contents lists Chance at p. 65 and Data at p. 66 where the folios are 64 and 65 — the body count closes exactly, so nothing is missing and the contents is one out; (9) Year 6's interior copyright and acknowledgement pages misname it the “Year 7” book — a copy-paste error in the book itself; its cover metadata, contents, running heads and body are Year 6 throughout, and it is read and placed as Year 6. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach — among them Year 3's conversion-table lines “1,000 cm = 1 km” (correct: 100,000 cm) and “10,000 mm = 1,000 m” (correct: 10 m), Year 4's banner “30 minutes = 1 hour” against its own body's 60, Year 5's “1 000 millimetres = 1 litre”, Year 9's contents “Common Multitudes” (for Common Multiples) and its (a × b)ᵐ = aᵐ × bⁿ misprint, and Year 1's count-in-twos strip printing 9 where 19 belongs; Year 10 carries its own printed errata list (pp. 201–202), whose corrections are noted in the items. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching point — a named concept, method, rule or worked skill with its printed page. The placements' named exclusions — strand divider pages; contents and acknowledgement pages; pure exercise sets and worked-example repeats of an item already placed; Year 1's “Checking my number” ten-frame reference page (printed p. 32); Year 3's duplicated capacity page (counted once); Year 4's closing multiplication-table chart (printed p. 86); Year 10's printed errata list (pp. 201–202, apparatus — its corrections ride in the items); Year 12's closing motivational quotes and exam tips (printed pp. 273–275); Year 13's bibliography and back matter — are stocked nowhere. Fiji COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Fiji book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 40 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Mathematics Year 1*), Book 2 (*Mathematics Year 2*), Book 3 (*Mathematics Year 3*), Book 4 (*Mathematics Year 4*), Book 5 (*Year 5 Mathematics*), Book 6 (*Year 6 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 7 (*Year 7 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 8 (*Mathematics Year 8*), Book 9 (*Mathematics Textbook Year 10*), Book 10 (*Mathematics for Year 11*), Book 11 (*Year 12 Mathematics Textbook*).

Printed page = PDF page − 2. Scan defect: printed p. 57 appears twice (PDF pp. 59–60 are the same leaf) and printed p. 58 is absent — the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand and is cited nowhere.

**Book 4:** *Mathematics Year 4* — Ministry of Education, Curriculum Development Unit, Revised 2015 (© 2013); 96,814,549 bytes, 86 PDF pp.; image-only scan read from rendered pages. Folios cited as printed in the book; the folio sequence is irregular (folios 44–48 and 54 never appear, and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice) — the leaf count closes exactly and every contents strand and unit is present, so the gaps are the book's own folio misprints, not missing leaves.

## Honduras added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Honduras's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **21 files = 18 distinct works: 9 pupil textbooks (Grades 3–9, Grade 10 Matemática I and Grade 11 Matemática III BCH) and 12 teacher-guide files for Grades 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 10 and 11, the Grade 8 guide held in two editions (2018 and the 2022 revision) and the Grade 1 and 2 guides each in two scans** — all from the Secretaría de Educación's PROMETAM / UPNFM series produced with JICA support — and placed 335 items (218 pupil-textbook items and 117 teacher-evidence items), plus 3 duplicate-edition records (placements items 241, 242 and 338) that are cited once with their works and carry no separate items. The expansion map's “9 student + 12 teacher” count is right: the staging `books.json` files, the `phase3-push-state.json` pushed set and `textbooks.tsv` agree on exactly these 21 files. There is no Grade 1–2 pupil textbook in the Secretaría's programme (workbook only — SE Manual de Lineamientos), so the Grade 1–2 teaching in hand is the two guides; there is no Grade 3 or Grade 6 teacher guide in hand. Every file was grabbed from its recorded `source_url` (educatrachos.hn / libroshonduras.com) after the staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, and verified by exact byte size against the download-list record and by page count against the `books.json` record before reading; grabbed copies were deleted after each book's text was extracted. Read basis, stated honestly: the six text-layer pupil books and eight text-layer guides were read in full from their text; the three scanned pupil books (Grades 7–9) were read by OCR of their Índices and unit/lesson openers with interior pages OCR-sampled — Grades 7 and 8 cross-checked against the full text-layer read of their own teacher guides, which plan every lesson class by class — and the two 2022 guides are themselves scans, OCR-read at cover, presentation and index level, the Grade 9 guide therefore placed at unit grain. Counting grain: one item per Índice lesson; a lesson teaching two distinct course topics is split into two items, and three items (a Grade 4 pupil lesson, a Grade 10 pupil lesson and a Grade 2 guide unit) each carry a row in two lessons below and are counted once. The placements' named exclusions — the “Páginas para recortar” cut-out pages of the Grades 3, 4 and 5 pupil books (manipulatives, not teaching text), the Grade 6 book's closing “Repaso” review, and the “Nos divertimos” / “¿Sabías que?” enrichment boxes inside placed lessons — are stocked nowhere. The read's flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) the download list §A's original 2026-10-21 mirror labels for the III-Ciclo files were swapped between Español and Matemáticas — the files it calls “Matemáticas 7/8/9 (2018 PROMETAM)” are the Español books, and its “Español 7/8/9” alternates are the Matemáticas books — a library-record fault, already corrected in `books.json`, reported here and not fixed by this merge; (2) the Grade 9 guide's cover misprints its cycle as “II Ciclo” where the programme is III Ciclo; (3) the Grade 11 pupil book's Índice misprints “Cincunferencias” for “Circunferencias”, taught corrected; (4) the 2014 scan of the Grade 1 guide has a materially poorer text layer than the mirror scan of the same work, so anyone text-mining it alone will under-read it. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach. Into this lesson the placements stock 15 items — 10 pupil-textbook rows and 5 teacher-evidence rows — from Book 1 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 3er grado*), Book 2 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 4to grado*), Book 3 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 5to grado*), Book 9 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 4to grado*), Book 10 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 5to grado*), Book 13 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 7mo grado (2018)*), Book 15 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 7mo grado*), Book 17 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 9no grado*), Book 18 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas and 9no grado (2022)*).

Teacher evidence only. The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Mozambique’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **15 files: 14 pupil Matemática modules of the ministry’s PESD distance-learning programme (PESD I Módulos 1–5, studied by the 1º ciclo = grades 7–9; PESD II Módulos 1–9, studied by the 2º ciclo = grades 10–12) and 1 teacher guide, the *Manual do Professor — 1.ª Classe*, whose Matemática part (printed pp. 146–183) is teacher evidence** — all from the Ministério da Educação e Desenvolvimento Humano (MINEDH) through its Instituto de Educação Aberta e à Distância (IEDA) — and placed 233 items (227 pupil-textbook items and 6 teacher-evidence items). Mozambique’s basis, by the user’s 2026-11-02 ruling, is this ministry distance-learning (PESD) set, not the commercial print set: the modules are studied by cycle, not per grade, and are cited as distance-learning books wherever they appear. The expansion map’s “14 student” count is exact, but its “0 teacher” is wrong — the teacher guide is filed under subject “general” at grade-01 and carries a full Matemática part; the true in-hand set is 14 pupil + 1 teacher guide. The three staging `books.json` files, `textbooks.tsv` and `phase3-push-state.json` agree on exactly these 15 files; no staging PDF remained on local disk (pusher-secured, local copies deleted), so every file was fetched fresh from its recorded official source, ieda.gov.mz, and matched its recorded byte size exactly; all 15 MD5s are distinct — no duplicates — and the generically named PESD II file `Modulo-9.pdf` is proved by its title page to be Matemática Módulo 9. Printed-page basis: PESD I modules — printed page = PDF page − 1 (footer-verified); PESD II modules — printed page = the contents-page foliation; the *Manual do Professor* — printed page = PDF page. Counting grain: one item per lesson of a module where the lesson teaches one course topic; a lesson is split into two items where it teaches two clearly distinct course topics. Book errors found on the read are taught corrected, as the placements record them: PESD II Módulo 3’s cover subtitle wrongly repeats Módulo 1’s (“Conjuntos Numéricos e Cálculo Algébrico”) — its interior is exponential and logarithmic functions throughout — and its answer section is headed “Soluções Modulo 1” (sic) although it is Módulo 3’s own key; PESD II Módulo 9’s answer section is headed “Soluções Módulo 8” (sic) although it is Módulo 9’s own key; PESD II Módulo 5’s contents numbers two different lessons “Lição 4” (the linear-function revision, printed p. 43, and the modulus function, printed p. 55) — both lessons are placed, as items 177 and 178 of this read; and PESD I Módulo 4’s contents page omits its Lição 13, which exists in the body at printed p. 64 and is placed (item 96), while its Lições 14 and 15 carry identical stated objectives and are placed as two items as printed (items 97 and 98). The placements’ record items are apparatus, stocked nowhere: R1 — every PESD module’s closing preparation test (teste de preparação), answer key (chave de correcção / soluções) and bibliography; R2 — the *Manual do Professor*’s answer key to the 1.ª Classe pupil book (printed pp. 174–183; the pupil book itself is not in hand); R3 — the DGD Módulo 1 spot-check copy, fetched only to verify its subject identity. The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: DGD (Desenho e Geometria Descritiva, PESD II Módulos 1, 3–12 — a separate named subject of the Mozambican curriculum with its own module series, interior-checked on Módulo 1 and excluded as such; Módulo 2 is not in hand at all); all other PESD subjects in hand (Biologia, Química, Física, Geografia, História, Inglês, Português, Francês, TIC, Agro-pecuária, Empreendedorismo, Educação Visual, Filosofia) — separate curriculum subjects, none a Matemática book in disguise; the *Manual do Professor*’s Língua Portuguesa and Educação Física parts (printed pp. 4–145 and 184–215); and the primary print-programme Matemática books (1ª–6ª, Edição revista 2025), which are link-only, not in hand and not read. Pupil items are COUNTED normally; the teacher-evidence items (T1–T6, from the one teacher guide) are named in the rows and stay outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items — 4 pupil-textbook rows — from Book 5 (*Matemática — Módulo 5 (PESD I)*).

**Book 5:** *Matemática — Módulo 5 (PESD I)* — Mozambique, Ministério da Educação e Desenvolvimento Humano (MINEDH), Instituto de Educação Aberta e à Distância (IEDA), 2023; 6,638,357 bytes, 172 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page − 1. Pupil textbook — a PESD distance-learning module, studied by cycle (1º ciclo = grades 7–9), not per grade.

## Nauru added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Nauru's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **10 documents, all of them Learning Through Doing (LTD) *Mathematics Planning Documents*, Nauru editions (2026) — Prep and Years 1–8, plus the LTD *Scope and Sequence, Years 1–8* — teacher planning alignments that sequence an Australian commercial subscription lesson system against the Nauru Mathematics Syllabus, every lesson keyed to a Nauru curriculum code (NRU-*); 0 pupil (student) textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 0 workbooks** — and placed 238 items, every one of them PLANNING/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE: the Nauru syllabus's own key-idea statements as printed on each document's closing “Nauru Curriculum Alignment” pages. Nauru publishes no government textbooks (country record, download list §§F–Q, seven fronts), so Nauru's standing in the course is the syllabus-evidence reckoning: Nauru is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure and is named in the countries line and in the rows below only, on the standing evidence-only precedent (Bahamas / Canada / Myanmar). The expansion map's count is right in number and wrong in kind: it carries Nauru as “10 student Maths books”, but every one of the 10 books.json entries is kind `planning-document` and states in its own approval note that it is NOT a Nauru government student textbook (diff fault F1 — a record-side fault, reported, not fixed by this merge). textbooks.tsv carries 12 Nauru rows, all of them USP IOE ESD items, and zero Mathematics rows — the 10 planning documents live only in books.json and push-state. Australia overlap is ZERO: the Australia read's nine Cambridge/Jacaranda Years 8–10 pupil textbooks share no title, ISBN, byte size or file with this set, and the QCE senior titles cross-referenced in Nauru's country record are link-only, in hand for neither country and read by neither reader; nothing from the Australia placements is cited here. All 10 files were read from local disk in phase3-staging, byte-exact against their books.json records (334,327–887,725 bytes; 67 PDF pages in total; 10 distinct MD5s, no duplicates inside the set); nothing was downloaded. Printed-page basis: the documents carry no printed folios (the Scope's footer “Page N” equals its PDF page), so pages cited are document (PDF) pages — Prep key ideas on alignment pp. 6–7 of 7, Years 1–8 on alignment p. 6 of 6; the term overviews (p. 1) and weekly plans (pp. 2–5) sequence the same key ideas and add none of their own. Counting grain: one item per NRU key idea. The *Scope and Sequence* (887,725 bytes, 13 PDF pages) was read in full and cross-checked — every Scope topic traces to a year document's lessons under the codes placed — so it contributes 0 items of its own; counting it again would double-count one programme as two sources. Book errors found on the read are placed corrected, as the placements record them (diff fault F2): Year 3 NRU-M3.3 prints “suing” for “using” and NRU-G3.3 prints “jey” for “key”; Year 5 NRU-CD5.4 prints “recoding sheets” for “recording sheets”; Year 8 NRU-PA8.4 prints “co-ordiinates”; the Year 8 alignment page prints its first geometry code as “NRU-G9.1” — a Year 9 code inside the Year 8 document — while the Year 8 weekly plans cite the same lessons as NRU-G8.1, and item 226 is placed as the Year 8 item it is; the Year 2 document's Term 4 plan cites “NRU-N3.4” against Year 2 strategy-review lessons, where the alignment page's NRU-N2.4 governs; several weekly-plan code strings are garbled in print — the alignment pages' code lists are clean and govern throughout. The placements also record a document inconsistency (diff fault F3): the Scope's fractions table opens its halves entry at the earliest (Prep) level, but the Prep planning document contains no fractions anywhere — fractions enter the year documents at Year 1 (NRU-N1.8) — and the placements follow the year documents. Some key ideas are explicitly Nauru-localised (Year 3 NRU-G3.3: simple maps of Nauru; Year 6 NRU-CD6.4: data contexts “familiar to Nauruan students”; Year 8 NRU-PA8.6: graphs “using data from Nauru”). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the Nauru Mathematics Syllabus aims paragraph printed in every year document (general aims, no subject content); the term overviews and weekly plans as separate items (they sequence the same key ideas, and the LTD lesson titles are the Australian system's commercial catalogue, delivered by subscription — the planning documents do not reproduce the lessons, so no lesson content is placed, only the syllabus key ideas the lessons are sequenced against); “Assessment Opportunities” headers and blank assessment columns (apparatus); the Scope & Sequence's duplicated topic statements across years (one programme, counted once at key-idea grain); the 12 USP IOE ESD files filed under Nauru (not Mathematics, excluded by subject); and the OER4Pacific COL open-schooling Mathematics 11/12 PDFs named in the download list §G (regional OER, never staged under Nauru, not in hand, not read). Into this lesson the placements stock 23 planning/curriculum-evidence items from Book 1 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Prep, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 2 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 1, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 3 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 2, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 4 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 3, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 5 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 4, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 6 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 5, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 7 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 6, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 8 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 7, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 9 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 8, Nauru edition (2026)*).

## Nigeria added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Nigeria’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **11 files: 1 pupil textbook and 10 teacher guides** — and placed 199 items (20 pupil-teaching items from the pupil textbook and 179 teacher-evidence items from the ten guides). The pupil textbook is *New Concept Mathematics for Senior Secondary Schools 1* (3rd ed. 2016, Learn Africa, 352 pp., 19,071,603 B), held as a release asset in `mid-size-books-8`; the guides are 7 Learn Africa guides (New Method Primary 1; New Concept JSS 1–3 and SS 1–3) and 3 Cambridge *Excellence in Mathematics* teacher’s guides (Books 1–3). The expansion map’s kind split (0 student + 10 teacher + 1 link-only) is wrong on the student count — the SS1 pupil textbook is in hand, and the map’s “1 link-only” is plausibly this same book misclassified; the true split is 1 student + 10 teacher, 11 files (flag F1, record-side). Record discrepancy (flag F2, record-side): the records describe the held JSS 1 teacher’s guide as a 2024 file of 396,871 B (4th edition), but the file in hand and read is the **2026 fifth edition** (362,534 B, ISBN 978 978 925 9755); the 2024 file is not in hand. Book errors found on the read are taught corrected (flag F3): the JSS 1 guide’s Ch20 states “1 hour = 1°” on the clock face — 1 hour = 30°; the JSS 2 guide prints “99 300 = 9.03 × 10⁴” (should be 9.93 × 10⁴), works a simple-interest example stated with T = 4 years at 3 years, and gives the Pythagorean triple “(5, 12, 23)” (should be (5, 12, 13)); the SS 1 guide prints the sector-area formula as θ/360 × 2πr² (should be θ/360 × πr²) and describes standard-form A as “between 0 and 9” (should be 1 ≤ A < 10); the SS 2 guide works log 0.000 592 and prints the characteristic without its negative/bar sign, and conflates “complementary” with “independent” events in places; the SS 3 guide calls A⁻¹ “the negative matrix” in one section (it is the inverse matrix, used correctly later), with several standard-integral lines and one index law garbled in print. The Primary 1 guide’s printed Contents page numbers do not match its body folios (flag F4) — its items cite body folios throughout. Printed-page basis: Learn Africa guide items cite the guide’s own printed (folio) pages — chapter starts from each book’s contents, verified against body folios; pupil-book items cite the pupil book’s printed pages; Cambridge items cite the guide’s printed topic-start page, each topic’s Student’s Book page span being given in the book’s scheme of work. Counting grain: one item per teaching chapter (Learn Africa books) or per teaching topic (Cambridge guides). The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: revision and exam-practice apparatus across the series (the SS1 pupil book’s Preparatory chapter and its three Revision-exercise parts; Cambridge TG1 Topics 1 and 16, TG2 Topics 1 and 18, TG3 Topics 1 and 13 and TG3’s sample examination papers); the SS 3 guide’s Ch16 “Numbers and numeration”, an explicit revision chapter; reference apparatus (the pupil book’s Tables and formulae, Four-figure tables, Answers and Index; the Cambridge guides’ Introduction sections); and the non-Maths Nigeria files checked and excluded (the English, Economics, Government, Commerce, Biology, Chemistry, Physics and Literature books in hand, the FME approved-textbooks list, and the 6 TVET trades curriculum documents — none is a Maths book). Pupil items are COUNTED normally; teacher-guide items are worded as teacher evidence and stay outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 12 items — 2 pupil-textbook rows and 10 teacher-evidence rows — from Books 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7 and 8.

**Book 1:** *New Method Mathematics for Primary Schools — Teacher's Guide 1* — Nigeria, Learn Africa; 230,103 bytes, 36 PDF pp. Teacher guide — teacher evidence only. Citations are the guide's own printed (folio) pages taken from the body: the book's printed Contents page numbers do not match its body folios (the contents uses a different pagination).

**Book 2:** *New Concept Mathematics for JSS 1 — Teacher's Guide*, 5th edition 2026 — Nigeria, Learn Africa, ISBN 978 978 925 9755; 362,534 bytes, 56 PDF pp. Teacher guide — teacher evidence only. The records describe the held copy as a 2024 file of 396,871 B (4th edition); that file is not in hand — the held and read copy is this 2026 fifth edition, and the placements are from it.

## Peru added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Peru's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **3 pupil WORKBOOKS of the Ministerio de Educación (MINEDU): *Cuaderno de trabajo Matemática* for Grades 4, 1 and 2** — and placed 238 items, one per *ficha* (the workbook's own numbered teaching unit). The expansion map's count is right and its kind label is wrong (record correction C1): the 3 are **workbooks**, not student textbooks — no Peru pupil *texto*, teacher guide or secondary Maths book is in hand, and every other Peru Maths title in the records (Primaria Matemática 3, 5, 6; Secundaria *Resolvamos problemas* 1–5; *Fichas de Matemática* 1–5; the multigrado/rural *textos*) is link-only, never downloaded. The fourth secured Peru file, the Grade 1 *Ciencia y Tecnología* fichas booklet (7 PDF pages, 18,486,216 bytes), was checked first-hand and is science only — no maths content — and is cited nowhere here. A workbook is a pupil book: every item below is **teaching content** (each ficha teaches its topic through worked situations, definitions in context and guided practice), so Peru's rows are pupil rows, COUNTED in full — the workbook set closes as a teaching set, not an evidence-only one (the South Africa pattern). The secured files are **complete volumes** (each runs from its cover to the standard MINEDU civics back matter at its printed end) and are not duplicates of one another (distinct byte sizes and MD5s). They are, though, **later printings than the records describe** (record correction C2): all three are the 2021 Dotación-2022 printings — Grade 4 the 5th edition of June 2021 (Depósito Legal BNP N.° 2021-08052), not the 2016 first edition of its download-list mirror note; Grade 1 likewise the 5th edition of June 2021 (Depósito Legal BNP N.° 2021-08267), not the 3rd edition of 2019 (Dotación 2020) its mirror note describes — and Grade 2's credits page carries both the 2019 printing (C. P. N.° 002-2019-MINEDU/VMGP/UE 120, Dotación 2020) and the 5th edition of June 2021 (Depósito Legal BNP N.° 2021-08050) over one shared reedition body; the citations below carry the printings actually read. ZNAJDUJE SIĘ NA KARCIE ROZWIĄZAŃ ZADAŃ OTWARTYCH”) with its point value (16: 0–2 · 17: 0–3 · 18: 0–2 · 19: 0–2 · 20: 0–3 · 21: 0–3); their statements live on the separate open-solutions card (8 pages, per the cover instruction), which is not in this file and not in hand — placements record item R1: tasks 16–21 are named only, no placement is made for any of them and no topic is inferred from the point values; if the card is ever secured, tasks 16–21 are owed a read and placements then. ## Jamaica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Jamaica's whole in-hand Maths set from the files — **8 documents: 7 National Standards Curriculum (NSC) guides that carry a Mathematics section (Grades 1, 2 and 3 Integrated Studies / Language Arts / Mathematics; Grades 4, 5 and 6 Exploratory Core; and the Grades 7–9 Mathematics guide — all Ministry of Education, Youth and Information, © 2018, the Grade 6 guide in its FINAL edition of Jan 08 2020), plus 1 pupil reader** — and placed 311 items: 308 curriculum-evidence items from the guides and 3 pupil-text items from the reader. **Kind split: 0 pupil textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 7 curriculum guides.** A curriculum guide states what is REQUIRED to be taught and how to teach it; it is not a pupil textbook, so every guide item below is CURRICULUM EVIDENCE, worded “curriculum requires”, and Jamaica is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure. The expansion map is wrong on both count and kind (flag F1, record-side, reported not fixed): it listed Jamaica as FILES-IN-HAND with 4 student / 0 teacher Maths books — its build matched the subject field only, counting the three Grades 1–3 guides and the Grades 7–9 guide and missing the three Grades 4–6 Exploratory Core guides, whose subject field reads only “Exploratory Core” while their title pages name Mathematics as one of the four core subjects; and the four files it did count are curriculum guides, not student books. Two further record faults ride with the set (record-side; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/jamaica.md filing exists or was created): textbooks.tsv has **no row at all for the Grades 7–9 Mathematics guide** (flag F2), although the file is in staging, in books.json and in the pushed set; and the TSV's Grade 5 row points at a Telegram link instead of the file's secured GitHub asset (flag F3) — the book was read byte-exact from that asset. One file defect, stated plainly: the Grade 5 guide's body font has a broken ToUnicode map, so its text layer extracts as bullet glyphs; its Mathematics section was read in full from rendered page images (120-DPI renders of PDF pp. 139–255 = printed pp. 122–238), every page legible, and it is counted as read on that basis. Printed-page basis: each guide prints its own folio; the Mathematics windows run printed = PDF − 5 (Grade 1), − 33 (Grade 2), − 16 (Grade 3), − 11 (Grade 4), − 17 (Grade 5) and − 12 (Grade 6), and Book 7's pages are its own per-grade section pages. Counting grain: Grades 1–3, one item per distinct numbered objective in the Mathematics Content Standards, plus NEW/Extended objectives and Scope-and-Sequence topics stated in no standard (marked (S&S)); Grades 4–6 and 7–9, one item per distinct objective in each Mathematics unit's objectives list; an objective restated verbatim is counted once, at its first full statement. Boundary files examined and excluded — stocked nowhere: the nursery *Birth to Three is Key* early-childhood guide (ages 0–3, all-domains; number concepts are one activity focus, with no Mathematics section or objectives at NSC grain — not a Maths book); the Grades 4–6 Resource & Technology guide (its file is a bundle whose opening leaves are the Grades 7–9 English guide; no Mathematics section); and the Civics (×2), English Language, Integrated Science and Social Studies guides (no Mathematics subject in any of them; the Science guide's measurement/graphing is science content). The placements' named exclusions inside the books are likewise stocked nowhere: the Grade 1 window's “Maths Prayers” (printed pp. 242–243 — devotional poems, religious apparatus, no mathematics content); the Scope and Sequence charts, Process Strand checklists, Illustrative Activities, Benchmarks and Illustrative Examples as apparatus (topics stated only there are placed as items and marked (S&S)); the Integrated Studies and Language Arts windows of the Grades 1–3 guides, which restate the same Mathematics attainment targets as delivery vehicles and state no requirement beyond the window's standards; the Grade 1 book's Mathematics lesson plans and “Teaching Mathematics: Ideas & Activities” (printed pp. 340–400, teacher apparatus); Book 7's Appendices A–E and closing sample lesson plan (printed pp. 191–206, activity apparatus and worked examples of the same objectives); and the reader's copyright/credits leaves. Book errors and faults are carried as the placements record them, taught corrected where they touch a row: the Grade 1 fractions standard (printed p. 275) cross-refers an objective as “Objective 2.3.27” — a Grade 2 bank number inside the Grade 1 book — and objective numbers with a leading “2.” recur throughout the Grade 1 standards (the bank numbering is shared across grades; each such objective is placed once, as stated for Grade 1); the Grade 2 addition example on printed p. 268 (“Pat has 43 oranges … How many apples Pat has now?”) mixes fruits mid-problem — a wording slip, the objective it illustrates placed as read; two Grade 2 standards pages carry a mojibake line (a Caesar-shifted “See previous page for Attainment Targets and Objectives”) — a print-production artefact, no content lost; the Grade 3 window and its Scope and Sequence state **no decimals** anywhere, so no Grade 3 decimals row is stocked; the Grade 4 Term 3 Unit 2 outcome names “cross-sections” of cubes/cuboids, which the unit nowhere teaches — placed inside its item as named-only; the Grade 4 probability unit's header prints “GRAGE 4” (sic); the Grade 5 Overview disagrees with its own units three times, recorded as printed (division of five-digit numbers sits in Term 3 Unit 1, not Term 2; the Overview's Volume has no objective anywhere in the Grade 5 Mathematics section and is stocked nowhere; lines of symmetry sit in Term 2 Unit 3, while the Term 3 Geometry unit is circles, polygons to eight sides, and pyramids); the Grade 6 Term 3 Unit 1 tyre-pressure activity is Science-linked and its arithmetic is already inside its unit's item, not placed separately; in Book 7, the Grade 9 ratio unit's footer prints “NSC Mathematics: Grade 7” (a template error — the unit sits in the Grade 9 section and is placed as Grade 9), the matrices unit header prints “GRADE 9 TERM 1: NUMBERS” although the unit sits in the Term 3 sequence (placed as Term 3), the Grade 7 constructions unit's objectives page repeats the transformations unit's first objective (counted once, in the transformations item), and the Grade 8 Term 2 geometry unit carries a header title copied from the algebra unit while its objectives are polygons and angle sums (placed by its objectives). Jamaica's pupil Maths textbooks are commercial (Carlong / Hodder / Macmillan etc.) — the library holds none; they stand in the download list only, not in hand and not read. Jamaica enters this lesson as curriculum evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count above — Jamaica's in-hand Maths set holds no pupil textbook and no teacher guide, only the seven National Standards Curriculum guides, which state what must be taught without being pupil teaching material themselves (the standing evidence-only precedent). Jamaica's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-only): every in-hand document has been read in full and stocked, and it advances only when a Jamaica pupil Maths textbook lands in hand and is read.

## Serbia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Serbia's whole in-hand Maths set from the file — **one pupil textbook**: *Математика 1* (Matematika 1 — Mathematics for the first grade of primary school), Нова школа (Nova škola), Belgrade, first edition 2018 — and placed **95 teaching items** from it. The book is a genuine, complete pupil textbook (уџбеник), not a workbook, not an exam paper and not an excerpt: every topic is presented, named and worked before it is practised. It is the only Serbia Maths book in hand — the expansion map's count and kind split (1 student, 0 teacher) are exact, and every record agrees. The file was read visually from full-page renders, every page, because its text layer is empty (a print-image PDF). It is the COMPLETE book: the CIP record gives 103 pages, the contents lists all seven units ending at printed p. 103, and the text runs printed pp. 1–103 followed by the imprint page (PDF p. 104). Printed-page basis: **printed page = PDF page** for the whole body, verified against printed folios throughout. Counting grain: one item per distinct taught skill or concept, at the point the book teaches it. The book's revision spreads (ОВО ЗНАМ / НАУЧИЛИ СМО) and its practice set (ЗАДАЦИ ЗА ВЕЖБАЊЕ) restate taught items and are named exclusions — except three places where a revision spread is the book's first presentation of new material (the number line and capacity-by-pouring on printed pp. 48–49, and balance-equivalence on printed p. 82), which are counted as teaching items and marked as such; in-lesson exercise sets are apparatus, not items. Also stocked nowhere: the front cover, contents, the seven unit divider pages and the imprint page, and the road-safety asides on printed p. 11 (no maths content). No book error was found in the book: its worked examples check out, and the contents page numbers agree with the printed folios throughout — a sheet-scale reading of printed p. 73 that seemed to omit 12 from the list of the numbers of the second ten was disproved on full-resolution re-check (the list is complete, 11, 12, 13, …, 20), an artefact of the reading scale, not a book error. Boundary, cited nowhere: Serbia's other 16 Maths files in the Kavčić free library (Grades 1–8 workbooks, textbooks and part-volumes, including the Grade 1 workbook *Математика 1 — радна свеска*) are link-only, not in hand and not read. One record-side note (reported, not fixed — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/serbia.md filing exists or was created): textbooks.tsv secures only 6 of the 7 pushed Serbia books — the Grade 4 Serbian grammar book is in books.json and in the push state but has no TSV row; it is not a maths book and does not affect this reckoning. Into this lesson the placements stock 2 rows — all from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Математика 1* (Matematika 1 — Mathematics for the first grade of primary school) (pupil textbook) — Serbia, Нова школа (Nova škola), Belgrade, first edition 2018; authors Милица Ћук, Бранислав Милошевић, Бранко Марковић; ISBN 978-86-6225-041-4; ministry approval решение 650-02-00208/2018-07 of 27. 4. 2018; 135,143,758 bytes, 104 PDF pp., MD5 9a5df48784a319517cff29b5b407d0b2. Complete book: printed pp. 1–103 + covers and imprint; printed page = PDF page throughout. Read visually from full-page renders (the file's text layer is empty). PUPIL TEXTBOOK — Serbia is counted in this lesson's taught-in figure.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| Counting the solids in a built-up picture and recording each count with dots or strokes in a table | Serbia — *Математика 1* (Grade 1 pupil textbook, Нова школа) — printed p. 18 |
| Showing a count by colouring one field of a grid per object, read against a dot scale (first pictogram) | Serbia — *Математика 1* (Grade 1 pupil textbook, Нова школа) — printed p. 38 |

Serbia enters this lesson as a counted country: its book is a genuine pupil textbook, so Serbia is named in the countries line, in the rows above, and in the acceptance count above, which this merge increments by one. Serbia's class for Maths is COURSES-CLOSED: the one in-hand book has been read in full and stocked, and the other Serbia Maths titles stand only as link-only records until they are secured and read.

## Haiti added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Haiti's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **28 files, every one of them teacher-side**: the nine *Programme à Compétences Minimales* (PCM) volumes for Années Fondamentales 1–9 (MENFP, Direction de l'Enseignement Fondamental, November 2019), the three IPN *Programme détaillé, Matières de base* volumes (4e, 5e, 6e année), the three *Programme Pédagogique Opérationnel* (PPO) Mathématiques fascicules for the 3e cycle (7e, 8e, 9e année, 1989-1990), the eight Nectar (MENFP) teacher sequences (four for 7e année, four for 8e année), the Nouveau Secondaire *Programme-Cadre* and the four NS detailed programmes (NS1–NS4) — every one of the 28 carrying a full Mathématiques section — and placed **1,639 items, every one of them TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE**: **Haiti has no pupil Maths textbook in hand under any label**, so Haiti is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure and is named in the countries line and in the rows below only, on the standing evidence-only precedent (Ghana / Bahamas / Canada / Myanmar). The expansion map's count for Haiti is wrong: it carries 15, counting only the books.json entries whose subject field reads “Mathematics”, and the download list claims 43 teacher files; the true maths-bearing set is 28 files — the nine “All subjects” PCM volumes, the three IPN detailed programmes and the NS Programme-Cadre all carry full maths sections the count missed (a record-side fault, reported, not fixed by this merge); textbooks.tsv is likewise a stale 28-row index naming only a subset of the secured files. Haiti's official pupil books (the MENFP/FAGEM *Manuels uniques* / “Mon manuel” series) are in hand for other subjects only, never for Mathematics, and the two remanié programme leads (a 7e année fascicule and a 4e année detailed programme) are not in hand — and programme documents are not textbooks under the 2026-11-10 textbook-test ruling in any case — so **Haiti stays PARTIAL for Maths, teacher evidence only**. All 28 files were read page-by-page from the secured PDFs in phase3-staging, byte-exact against their books.json records; nothing was downloaded. Read depth: the PPO 8e fascicule and the IPN 4e and 6e programmes are image-only scans and were read by OCR in full, spot-checked against the page images — where OCR garbled a worked example's digits, the placement teaches the corrected arithmetic and the case is flagged; PPO 7e PDF p. 48 and PPO 9e PDF pp. 20, 30, 31 and 32 were verified blank, separator or title pages carrying no objectives. Printed-page bases: PCM printed page = PDF page − 1; IPN 4e and 6e printed page = PDF page − 9, IPN 5e printed folios equal PDF pages; PPO 7e printed = PDF − 3, PPO 8e printed folio = PDF page, PPO 9e printed = PDF − 2; Programme-Cadre NS printed = PDF − 9 (verified against the detailed programme at §5.1–§5.2); NS1 and NS2 printed = PDF page, NS3 cites PDF pages, NS4 printed = PDF − 1; the Nectar sequences cite PDF pages by séance. The placements' eleven flags are all document-side and are carried here exactly: the PCM 6e volume's maths section header mislabels its level “5ème AF” (F1 — its items are cited to the 6e volume they stand in); PCM 5e's spoken-number row slips (“six mille deux” for 6,002, and a thousands-family slip in item 352's row) are placed corrected (F2); the PCM “orthocentre” definition error stands in the PCM **7e** volume — the diff's “(AF8)” label is the diff's own slip, disproven against the live PCM 8e section, which carries the correct circumcentre definition (F3); PCM 9e's right-asymptote and strict-inequality rows are completions of points the volume leaves silent, not errors (F4); PCM 9e's opening “années diagnostiques” ((6e), 7e, 8e) reinstate the diagnostic year the 3e-cycle fascicules dropped (F5); the PCM 7e volume announces a simple-interest objective that does not exist anywhere in its grids — nothing is placed for it, and simple interest is taught by PCM 9e and PPO 9e (F6); the Nectar “Relations entre les angles” sequence states a false corresponding-angles criterion and a mise en train with no solution, and the “Calcul littéral” sequence's séance 5 example drops a factor of x — all placed as corrected (F7, F8); the record-side faults (the map's count, the download list's count, the stale textbooks.tsv) are reported, not fixed (F9); the IPN 6e worked examples carry numerical inconsistencies (objectives 5.1, 7.1, 12.2, 16.1, 19.1, 21.1, 22.1) flagged as read and NOT asserted as print errors — the volume is an OCR-read scan and some may be digit misreads (F10); and NS2's own dating (2015 in the detailed programmes' shared headers, the school year 2008-2009 on its cover) is the book's own, not a discrepancy (F11). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the PCM volumes' other-discipline sections and their week-by-week scheduling columns beyond each distinct objective; the IPN volumes' maths avant-propos, theme indexes, other-subject sections and Suggestions/Évaluation apparatus (except where an activity is itself the taught content); the PPO fascicules' shared front matter, Programme-Cadre theme tables, progression grids, bibliographies and annexes; the Nectar sequences' summative-evaluation séances and session-management apparatus (except where a séance's activity is the taught content); the Programme-Cadre's other subjects, and the Nouveau Secondaire framework chapters shared between it and the NS1/NS2 volumes (placed once, from the Programme-Cadre volume); and the verified blank and separator pages. Into this lesson the placements stock 22 items — all teacher/curriculum-evidence rows — from Book 1 (*PCM, 3ème AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 2 (*PCM, 4e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 3 (*PCM, 7e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 4 (*PCM, 8e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 5 (*PCM, 9e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 6 (*IPN Programme détaillé, 4e année*), Book 7 (*PPO Mathématiques, 7e année (1989-1990)*), Book 8 (*PPO Mathématiques, 8e année (1989-1990)*), Book 9 (*PPO Mathématiques, 9e année (1989-1990)*), Book 10 (*Nectar «Traitement des données» (7e année)*), Book 11 (*Programme-Cadre NS (MENFP)*), Book 12 (*NS1 Programme détaillé (MENFP)*), Book 13 (*NS2 Programme détaillé (MENFP)*), Book 14 (*NS3 Programme détaillé (MENFP)*) and Book 15 (*NS4 Programme détaillé (MENFP)*).

## Suriname added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Suriname's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **18 books: 13 pupil Maths books, 3 teacher Maths books and 2 kindergarten curriculum books** (20 files examined; two further in-hand files, the LJ3 OJW *Leerlingenwerkboek* and *Ik-boek*, were checked and excluded — drawing and orientation activities with no rekenen strand and no number teaching) — and placed **376 items: 344 pupil teaching items, 7 teacher-evidence items and 25 curriculum-evidence items**. No Suriname row existed in any maths lesson before this merge, so every placed item is new here. **Set verdict, proved from the books themselves:** the expansion map's count is right for the Mathematics-labelled set (13 pupil + 3 teacher = 16 files), but its kind labels are wrong in three places: (1) the LJ6 “Leerkrachtenhandleiding Deel 1” of the records is the programme's **Algemene Inleiding** volume (Book 14), not a lesson-by-lesson guide to Deel 1; (2) the two LJ7 teacher books are **oplossingenboeken — answer keys** (Books 15–16), teacher evidence; (3) Book 4 is a **rekenboek** (lesson book), not the “Leerlingenboek” textbooks.tsv names it — the same book. **Maths was hiding under another subject label once:** the KG *Spelend leren* Themaboek 3 and 4 (Books 17–18), filed as “Play-based learning” with textbooks.tsv kind “student” — also wrong: they are **teacher curriculum books** — each carry twelve *voorbereidend rekenen* (preparatory maths) sections with stated learning goals; their 24 items are stocked as **curriculum evidence**, never pupil teaching. **Partial volumes, named as such:** Books 4 and 5 are **Deel A only** and Book 12 is **Deel 1 only**; the download list's remark that LJ8 Deel 2 is “already held” is **not borne out by any record in hand** (textbooks.tsv, the staging books.json and the push-state carry only Deel 1 for LJ8) — LJ8 Deel 2 is treated as not in hand, and the LJ4/LJ5 B-volumes and LJ8 Deel 2–3 are link-only or unrecorded, cited nowhere. Book 8 (LJ6 Deel 3) is staged and pushed but has no textbooks.tsv row — the map counted it, the TSV does not carry it. **The six flagged claims are carried exactly:** (F1) **Book 11 (LJ7 Deel 3) has no Les 24** — contents and body both jump from Les 23 (tangram) to Les 25 (tekenen op schaal); the book holds 38 lessons, not 39, and the rows cite the 38 that exist; (F2) the LJ8-Deel-2 “already held” claim, above — contradicted, not in hand; (F3) **book error, Book 6 Les 6 (printed p. 23):** the information block adds a quarter hour to 15.45 and gives the result as “06:00” — the correct result is **16:00**, and the row teaches the corrected form; (F4) **book error, Book 7 Les 14 (printed p. 54):** the information states the area of a rectangle with a garbled formula line (the perimeter formula 2l + 2b is given correctly, and the area is worked by counting in the same lesson) — the row teaches perimeter = 2l + 2b and area by counting/calculation, the forms the lesson's own worked examples use; (F5) **book error, Book 6 Les 30 (printed p. 93):** the split-subtraction check line prints minus signs where the method needs the parts added (7,986 − 5,234 split as 2,000 + 700 + 50 + 2 = 2,752) — the method itself is taught correctly in Les 21 and the rows follow the correct form; (F6) **Book 13's kind:** textbooks.tsv files the LJ10 Reader as a pupil book; its foreword states it is a guide “voor de reken-leerkracht en leerling” (for teacher and pupil) — its items are placed as teaching content, worded as a **programme reader**, not a pupil textbook. **Text-layer caveat (not a claim):** Book 3's text layer drops the digit “1” in several headings (“splitsingen van 0” for the splitting of 10); the rows use the true values, read from the sums and context. **Checked and NOT flagged:** the answer keys (Books 15–16) match their pupil books lesson for lesson (39 and 38 lessons respectively), and Book 14's contents listing of the kwartaal-1 lessons matches Book 6. Byte sizes checkable against the gov.sr records matched exactly (Books 7, 8, 10, 11). Printed-page basis: printed page = PDF page in every book except Books 17–18 (printed = PDF − 5). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the *Vooruit* books' week-closing revision pages (*weekafsluitingen*); Books 1–3's *herhaling* revision worksheets, *oefentoets* practice tests, *knipbladen* cut-out sheets and automatisation games, except where a sheet first teaches a point (those are placed); the *Plezier* books' closing games; Book 13's ch. 1 programme tables beyond item R1; and Books 17–18's non-maths theme sections. The *Plezier in rekenen* companion volumes found by the 2026-11-10 gov.sr census (LJ4 B leerlingenboek, LJ4 werkboek/takenboek, LJ5 takenboek/bijwerkboek) are link-only finds, not in hand — not read, not counted. Suriname counts in a lesson's taught-in figure only on the strength of its pupil rows; its teacher rows and curriculum rows are evidence — cited as the evidence kind they are and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent). Into this lesson the placements stock 21 items (21 pupil) from Book 6 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 1 (LJ6)*), Book 7 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 2 (LJ6)*), Book 8 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 3 (LJ6)*), Book 10 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 2 (LJ7)*), Book 11 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 3 (LJ7)*) and Book 12 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 1 (LJ8)*).

**Book 6:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Deel 1, leerlingenboek, Leerjaar 6* (pupil textbook) — Suriname, Ministerie van Onderwijs, Wetenschap en Cultuur (MinOWC), 2023 (made with VUB); 4,251,212 bytes, 96 PDF pp. Pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page. Kwartaal 1, lessons 1–30.

**Book 7:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Deel 2, leerlingenboek, Leerjaar 6* (pupil textbook) — Suriname, MinOWC, 2023; 5,713,419 bytes, 144 PDF pp. Pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page (byte size matches the gov.sr record exactly). Kwartaal 2, lessons 1–39.

**Book 8:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Deel 3, leerlingenboek, Leerjaar 6* (pupil textbook) — Suriname, MinOWC, 2023; 2,709,444 bytes, 136 PDF pp. Pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page (byte size matches the gov.sr record exactly). Kwartaal 3, lessons 1–39. (The book is staged and pushed but carries no textbooks.tsv row — a record gap, reported, not fixed by this merge.)

Kwartaal 1, lessons 1–30. **Only Deel 1 in hand for LJ8** — the download list's remark that LJ8 Deel 2 is “already held” is contradicted by every record (textbooks.tsv, the staging books.json and the push-state carry Deel 1 only); Deel 2 is treated as not in hand.

## Papua New Guinea added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Papua New Guinea’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **32 files = 28 works, all Papua New Guinea Department of Education (NDoE), “Issued free to schools by the Department of Education”: 8 pupil textbook files (7 works — the JICA/QUIS-ME National Mathematics Textbooks, Grades 1–6, with Grades 3 and 5 in two parts each) and 24 teacher files (21 works — 9 JICA teacher’s manual files, 6 works with Grades 3–5 in two parts each, and 15 Standards Based Curriculum (SBC) teacher guides, Elementary Prep to Grade 12, with separate Advanced and General guides at Grades 11 and 12)** — and placed 503 items (102 from the pupil textbooks, 401 teacher-evidence items from the manuals and guides). The expansion map is wrong on both of its numbers (flag 1 — record-side, reported, not fixed by this merge): it said 6 student + 22 teacher in hand; the true set is 8 student + 24 teacher. The map missed three files that exist only as textbooks.tsv rows (the Grade 4 and Grade 6 full-year JICA pupil textbooks and the Grade 6 JICA manual) and did not know the Grade 2 SBC guide, which has a staging books.json row but no textbooks.tsv row and no push-state entry (flag 4). **Filing error (flag 2 — record-side): the git-tree copies recorded for the Grade 1 and Grade 2 JICA manuals are a different country’s book** — both TSV tree paths hold the identical file, a Mozambique Grade 1 teacher manual in Portuguese (“Manual do Professor, 1.ª classe”, 5,891,019 B / 216 pp, the same MD5 at both paths — the only byte-identical duplicate pair found in this hunt, and not a PNG book), which also sits in the release `mid-size-books-8` correctly named as a Mozambique book. The genuine PNG manuals were read from the GitHub release `full-quality-oversized-books`, whose assets match the staged sizes and checklist MD5s exactly; the push-state’s per-folder landing claims for those two files should be treated as unreliable until the tree paths are re-filed. Flag 3: the two manuals’ recorded education.gov.pg source URLs do not serve the staged pressings — both returned an HTML page during the read, and a later re-probe served a different pressing of the Grade 1 manual — so the staged pressings cannot be re-grabbed from the recorded source URLs. Flag 5: the Grade 9 SBC guide’s textbooks.tsv tree path 404s; the file was recovered by searching all release assets and matches books.json on size and pages. No Maths hides under other subject labels in the PNG records — all 156 staging books.json entries and all 163 textbooks.tsv rows were checked by subject, title and path. Link-only Maths not in hand and not read (it does not count against closure): the FODE distance course-books for Grades 7–12 Mathematics / Maths A / Maths G (unit PDFs) and the two OBE upper-secondary guides found 2026-11-10 (Advanced Mathematics TG, ISBN 978-9980-9924-8-2; General Mathematics TG). Book errors found on the read are taught corrected: the Grade 1 Textbook and its manual print “Subtracation” in the Chapter/Unit 12 title; the SBC guides print “Angels” for Angles in places, “Algebrain” for Algebraic (a Grade 11 guide’s Factorisation unit header) and “Orobability” for Probability (the Grade 9 guide’s strand table); the Grade 1 SBC guide’s Term-1 record prints “tenths” where tens is meant. The Grade 3 SBC programme has no Money topic where the JICA Grade 3 textbook carries one — a programme difference, not an error. Printed-page basis: JICA textbook pages cite the book’s own printed folio / contents page; JICA manuals cite the manual’s own printed contents page; SBC guides cite the guide’s printed programme/contents pages (the basis is stated in each book entry below); where a contents extraction was partly two-column-interleaved, the PDF page is given as well and marked “PDF p.”. Counting grain: one item per chapter (pupil textbooks), per unit (teacher’s manuals — the manual unit mirrors the textbook chapter of the same number and is counted once, as teacher evidence), and per named topic/unit in the SBC guides’ own programmes. The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the end-of-book/year “Summary” chapters and units in the JICA textbooks and manuals (review apparatus), and the SBC guides’ front sections, assessment-record templates, glossaries/resources and integrated cross-subject project sections. Pupil-textbook rows are COUNTED normally; every manual and SBC guide row is teacher evidence — named in the countries line and in the rows, but outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 17 items — 5 pupil-textbook rows and 12 teacher-evidence rows — from Book S1 (*National Mathematics Textbook Grade 1*), Book S2 (*National Mathematics Textbook Grade 2*), Book S4 (*National Mathematics Textbook Grade 3, Part 2*), Book S5 (*Mathematics Grade 4 Textbook*), Book T1 (*Mathematics Teacher's Manual Grade 1*), Book T2 (*Mathematics Teacher's Manual Grade 2*), Book T4 (*National Mathematics Teacher's Manual Grade 3, Part 2*), Book T5 (*National Mathematics Teacher's Manual Grade 4, Part 1*), Book T6 (*National Mathematics Teacher's Manual Grade 4, Part 2*), Book T10 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary Prep (SBC)*), Book T11 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary One (SBC)*), Book T13 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 3 (SBC)*), Book T14 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 4 (SBC)*), Book T21 (*Advanced Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 11 (SBC)*), Book T22 (*General Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 11 (SBC)*).

**Book S1:** *National Mathematics Textbook Grade 1* (pupil textbook) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME), 2024; 33,202,520 bytes, 184 PDF pp. PUPIL TEXTBOOK — counted. Printed folio = the book’s contents page (Chapter 1, printed p. 8, is PDF p. 16; printed = PDF − 8 in the body).

**Book S2:** *National Mathematics Textbook Grade 2* (pupil textbook) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME); 33,863,464 bytes, 236 PDF pp. PUPIL TEXTBOOK — counted. Printed folio = the book’s contents page; chapter openers verified against the book’s own contents list.

**Book S5:** *Mathematics Grade 4 Textbook* (pupil textbook, full-year JICA file) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME); 18,154,613 bytes, 244 PDF pp. PUPIL TEXTBOOK — counted. Chapters 1–20 in one file. Printed pages from the book’s contents (printed = PDF − 8 in the body). A textbooks.tsv-only row — the file has no staging books.json entry (flag 1).

**Book T1:** *Mathematics Teacher’s Manual Grade 1* (teacher’s manual) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME); 152,741,463 bytes, 344 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — the manual reproduces the pupil pages with lesson flow, objectives and assessment for the same content. Its 17 units mirror the Grade 1 Textbook’s chapters 1–17; manual printed pages are the manual’s own contents pages. Read from the GitHub release `full-quality-oversized-books` copy, whose size and MD5 match the staged checklist exactly: the textbooks.tsv git-tree path recorded for this manual holds a Mozambique Grade 1 teacher manual instead (flag 2), and the manual’s recorded education.gov.pg source URL does not serve this staged pressing (flag 3).

**Book T2:** *Mathematics Teacher’s Manual Grade 2* (teacher’s manual) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME); 154,818,560 bytes, 400 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — as Book T1. Its 17 units mirror the Grade 2 Textbook’s chapters 1–17; manual printed pages are the manual’s own contents pages. Read from the GitHub release `full-quality-oversized-books` copy, MD5-matched to the staged checklist: the textbooks.tsv git-tree path recorded for this manual holds the same Mozambique book as T1’s path (flag 2), and its recorded source URL does not serve this staged pressing (flag 3).

**Book T4:** *National Mathematics Teacher’s Manual Grade 3, Part 2* (teacher’s manual) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME); 14,847,377 bytes, 170 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — as Book T3. Part 2 = Units 11–20.

**Book T5:** *National Mathematics Teacher’s Manual Grade 4, Part 1* (teacher’s manual) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME); 15,526,873 bytes, 180 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — the manual guides the Grade 4 Textbook, Units 1–20 across its two parts. Part 1 = Units 1–10; manual printed pages are the manual’s own contents pages.

**Book T6:** *National Mathematics Teacher’s Manual Grade 4, Part 2* (teacher’s manual) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME); 13,393,351 bytes, 176 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — as Book T5. Part 2 = Units 11–20.

**Book T10:** *Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary Prep* (SBC teacher guide) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), Standards Based Curriculum, 2015; 6,660,506 bytes, 206 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — scripted daily lesson plans (printed pp. 23–184); the topics stocked here are the guide’s own, from its Planning and Programming tables (printed pp. 8–22) and its Assessment strand tables (printed pp. 185–187). No SBC pupil textbook is in hand at any grade.

Book error noted on the read: the Term-1 record prints “tenths” where tens is meant in the place-value breakdown of 24.

**Book T13:** *Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 3* (SBC teacher guide, Junior Primary) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), Standards Based Curriculum; 24,810,344 bytes, 140 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY. Extent: unlike the elementary guides, this guide carries sample guided lessons only (printed pp. 14–124), not a full year of daily plans — the topic list stocked here is the full year’s programme, from the guide’s Yearly Overview (printed pp. 10–13). Noted on the read: this SBC programme has no Money topic, where the JICA Grade 3 textbook carries one — a programme difference, not an error.

The Advanced guide shares the General guide’s Numeracy, Measurement, Trigonometry, Geometry, Factorisation, Equations and Data units, adds a Functions and Graphs unit, and has no Financial Mathematics unit. The Grade 11 guides print “Algebrain” for Algebraic in the Factorisation unit header — a misprint, taught corrected.

The guide prints “Algebrain” for Algebraic in its Factorisation unit header — a misprint, taught corrected.

## Sweden added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Sweden's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 files, 594 PDF pages**, of which **10 carry mathematics**: the national curriculum Lgr22, 2 SPSM pupil materials, and 7 SPSM teacher guides, notes and support publications. Sweden has **no state pupil textbook series** — textbook approval was abolished in 1991, Skolverket sets the curriculum (Lgr22), and the state producer SPSM (Specialpedagogiska skolmyndigheten) publishes special-needs materials — so the pupil teaching in hand is SPSM material for blind and deaf pupils, and nothing else: 13 pupil items (the Abakus exercise book and the Växelspelet money game), 81 teacher-evidence items and 79 curriculum-evidence items from Lgr22's Matematik kursplan, 173 items in all. The other 3 files in the read set prove on reading to carry no maths at all and are stocked nowhere: *Skrivmaterial för elever som behöver förstärkta linjer och rutor* is blank ruled/grid stationery (copying masters), the *ABC Skriva-Läsa* teacher guide is literacy only, and a generically named *Arbetsblad* worksheet (pharmacy vocabulary) was pulled in as a hidden-maths spot check and cleared negative. The expansion map is wrong on both axes (flag F4): it listed 8 files as 3 student + 5 teacher, but two of its eight are not Maths at all (F1: the Skriva-Läsa guide, filed in books.json under “Matematik; Svenska”, is a literacy guide — its only number-adjacent moment, drawing a numbered slip 2–5 to pick that many pictures (printed p. 6), is classroom apparatus; F3: the Skrivmaterial, counted as a student Maths book, is blank stationery), a third is the wrong kind (F2: *Räkna med mig*, filed as a student book, is a 105-page handbook for teachers of braille-using pupils — all 35 of its items are teacher evidence), and four maths-bearing files were missed by the map entirely — Lgr22 itself (its section 5.5 is the full Matematik syllabus), the *Arbeta med språkstörning i matematikundervisningen* support publication (filed under generic special education), *Växelspelet* (filed under “Verklighetsuppfattning”) and the *Jag handlar* teacher guide (filed under home-economics subjects). Flags F1–F4 are record/map-side: they are reported here and carried in this section; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/sweden.md filing exists. The books' own errors are carried corrected: F5 — both Abakus volumes misprint their print ISBNs with a “987-” prefix (Övningsbok “987-91-28-11602-2”, Handledning “987-91-28-11600-8”; each book's PDF ISBN carries the correct 978- prefix); F6 — *Räkna med mig*'s ISBN is printed malformed as “98-91-28-10386-2”; F7 — the Abakus Handledning's division chapter (printed p. 45) says the pupil must master “multiplikation” where the sense requires division, taught corrected on the division row it touches; F8 — a box in *Räkna med mig* (printed p. 38) misnames the Abakus handbook as “Räkna med mig – handledning”. The placements' record items are stocked nowhere: R1 (Lgr22's assessment criteria, printed pp. 60–63 — they restate the central content as standards and add no content beyond the items), R2 (the Abakus Handledning's exercise sets and maths-card copying masters), R3 (the Arbeta publication's overview material), R4 (*Räkna med mig*'s apparatus), R5 (the Diamanterna card game itself is a physical product, not a book, and is not in hand — only its one-page SPSM note is) and R6 (the *Jag handlar* pupil book is recorded only as a sample that was never staged as a secured file; only its teacher guide is in hand). One basis note: the Övningsbok's multiplication and division exercise pages are abacus diagrams with no printed numerals, so those two placements rest on the book's contents page, section headings and introduction, exactly as the placements state. Sweden's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-heavy)**: every in-hand Maths-bearing file has been read and stocked, but 160 of its 173 items are teacher or curriculum evidence and its pupil side is special-needs material only. Into this lesson the placements stock 6 items: 3 curriculum evidence, 3 teacher evidence. No Sweden pupil item stocks into this lesson, so Sweden is added to the countries line above and named in the rows below as evidence only, and is NOT added to the acceptance count, on the standing evidence-only precedent.

**Book 1:** *Läroplan för grundskolan, förskoleklassen och fritidshemmet 2022* (Lgr22) (national curriculum) — Sweden, Skolverket, 2024; 1,317,913 bytes, 272 PDF pp., MD5 ea52068ef1b63a71f80885ddc7abdf95. Read for its Matematik kursplan in full (section 5.5, printed pp. 54–63) plus the förskoleklass chapter's maths content; the book's other 18 subject syllabi are out of scope for a Maths read. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — Lgr22 states what must be taught and teaches nothing itself; its rows are worded as the requirements they are and are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure. Its assessment criteria (printed pp. 60–63) restate the central content as standards and are record item R1, stocked nowhere.

**Book 9:** *Arbeta med språkstörning i matematikundervisningen* (support publication) — Sweden, SPSM, 2021, ISBN 978-91-28-00992-8; 764,290 bytes, 26 PDF pp., MD5 60b56c2eb928a6983dceccca12e03797. SPSM support publication on the language of mathematics for pupils with developmental language disorder. It teaches no pupil maths directly; the items placed from it are the concrete maths content it names, demonstrates or uses as worked material, placed as teacher evidence. Its overview material — the catalogue of known difficulty points (printed p. 4), its concrete-versus-abstract discussion (p. 9) and its general support catalogue (pp. 15, 17) — is record item R3, stocked nowhere. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 10:** *Räkna med mig* (teacher handbook) — Sweden, SPSM, 2018, best.nr 10386; 20,465,920 bytes, 105 PDF pp., MD5 5fbeac506f239001f466a59aa7574d9e. A 105-page handbook on mathematics teaching for compulsory-school pupils who use braille, addressed to teachers (foreword, printed p. 7) and built on the Lgr11 central content; grabbed from its recorded source_url after the GitHub Contents API returned 404. Filed in the records as a student book — wrong (flag F2): it is not a pupil book, and all 35 of its items are teacher evidence. Book errors: its ISBN is printed malformed as “98-91-28-10386-2” (flag F6), and a box on printed p. 38 misnames the Abakus handbook as “Räkna med mig – handledning” (flag F8); the Abakus guide placed from Book 4 is the real book. Its apparatus — the accessible-learning-environment and adapted/national tests chapters (printed pp. 14–26), the tools and materials catalogue (pp. 85–91), the maths word list (p. 95), the table of common signs in maths braille (pp. 96–97) and the copying masters (pp. 98–103) — is record item R4, stocked nowhere. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

Sweden enters this lesson as evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count — no Sweden pupil item stocks into this lesson, and its rows here are teacher-guide and curriculum evidence (the standing evidence-only precedent). Sweden's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-heavy): the in-hand set is read and stocked in full, but its pupil side is SPSM special-needs material only and there is no mainstream pupil textbook in hand.
