## Switzerland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Switzerland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **8 files / 6 works, and no pupil textbook among them**: the three PER brochures *Mathématiques et Sciences de la nature / Sciences humaines et sociales*, Cycles 1, 2 and 3 (CIIP, Version 3.0, août 2024), whose Mathématiques parts are placed here; the Ticino *Piano di studio della scuola dell'obbligo ticinese* (DECS, edizione perfezionata 2022), whose Matematica chapter is placed here; the *Lehrplan 21 Graubünden* (Fassung vom 15.03.2016), whose Mathematik plan is placed here — one work in three filed language editions (German, Romansh, Italian), read in full in the German edition, verified identical in structure and pagination in the other two, and cited once; and one teacher guide, *MaMa — Linee guida del progetto MaMa: Ambito Numeri e calcolo* (DECS/CeRDD, 2021), whose didactic rulings are placed here as teacher evidence — 425 items in all (364 curriculum evidence + 61 teacher-guide evidence, 0 pupil). The expansion map's kind split is WRONG (record-level; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records): it recorded "3 student + 1 teacher", but the three "student" files are the PER **curriculum** brochures mislabelled — **Switzerland has 0 pupil Maths textbooks in hand**, and its prescribed pupil series (Schweizer Zahlenbuch, Mathematik Primarstufe / Sekundarstufe I (LMVZ), Mathwelt, Mathbuch, MER Mathématiques 1–11, Ticino's Base Matematica 1–4) are commercial and licence-gated, not in hand and not read. Further maths was found hiding under "all subjects" labels: the Ticino Piano and the Lehrplan 21 Graubünden are all-subjects volumes carrying full mathematics chapters. Every grabbed file was verified SHA-256 byte-exact against its books.json record. Checked in hand and excluded with reasons, stocked nowhere: the three PER *Aperçus des contenus* (one example-grain maths page each — record items R1–R3), the PER *Présentation générale* (framework structure only), the PER *Éducation numérique* brochure (the computer-science curriculum; mathematics appears only as cross-links — a separate school subject) and the four Vaud *<DÉ>CODAGE* digital-education teacher manuals (contents pages of all four read: Science informatique / Médias / Usages only — a separate school subject); inside the PER brochures, the Sciences de la nature and Sciences humaines et sociales parts are other subjects, the *Modélisation* objectives are process apparatus whose content is carried by the per-theme problem-solving items, and the Lexiques are apparatus; inside the Ticino volume, every chapter other than Matematica is another subject and the process/attitude competence targets carry no subject content; inside the Lehrplan 21 volumes, every subject other than Mathematik is out of scope and the Einführung/Gesamtüberblick chapters are structure, not content; MaMa §§1 and 3 are project apparatus, and §2's quoted Ticino targets are placed at the Ticino items, not double-counted (record item R4). One within-document inconsistency is flagged so it is not stocked: the PER Cycle 1 *Aperçus des contenus* claims Cycle 1 pupils "calculate lengths (perimeter of a rectangle), areas, volumes (cube)" — the governing Cycle 1 brochure requires only comparison and measurement with an étalon (items 46–55), and perimeter/area/volume calculation begins in Cycle 2 (items 158–160); the brochure governs, the Aperçu's Cycle 1 sentence is an overstatement, and it is NOT stocked as a Cycle 1 requirement (record item R1). One document error is called out by a source itself and taught corrected: the MaMa guide rules that there is no "dissociative property" of addition — splitting an addend and associating (16 + 19 = 16 + 10 + 9) is the associative property, and textbooks that name it otherwise are wrong (item 378); the guide's other flagged classroom errors are misconceptions it warns against, not errors it commits. No Liechtenstein file is judged here: Liechtenstein teaches from Switzerland's books, but no file is filed under Liechtenstein in any record. Printed-page basis: PER brochures, Ticino Piano and Lehrplan 21 — printed folio = PDF page − 2; MaMa — printed page = PDF page. Switzerland's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**: every in-hand Maths-bearing file has been read and stocked, but all 425 of its items are curriculum or teacher-guide evidence and no pupil textbook is in hand. Into this lesson the placements stock 9 items (5 curriculum evidence and 4 teacher evidence) from Book 3, Book 4, Book 5 and Book 6. Switzerland is added to the countries line and named in the rows below as evidence only, and is NOT added to the acceptance count, on the standing evidence-only precedent.

**Book 3:** *Mathématiques et Sciences de la nature / Sciences humaines et sociales — Cycle 3* (curriculum brochure) — Switzerland, CIIP (Conférence intercantonale de l'instruction publique de la Suisse romande et du Tessin), Plan d'études romand (PER), Version 3.0, août 2024; 1,241,996 bytes, 116 PDF pp.; SHA-256 verified exact against its books.json record. Printed folio = PDF p. − 2. Only the Mathématiques part (printed pp. 14–35) is placed here; the Sciences de la nature and Sciences humaines et sociales parts of the same brochure are other subjects. Filed in the records as a student book — wrong: it is a curriculum brochure. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the brochure states what must be taught and teaches nothing itself; its rows are worded as the requirements they are and are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 4:** *Piano di studio della scuola dell'obbligo ticinese* (cantonal curriculum) — Switzerland, Repubblica e Cantone Ticino, Dipartimento dell'educazione, della cultura e dello sport (DECS), edizione perfezionata 2022; 2,789,910 bytes, 266 PDF pp.; SHA-256 verified exact against its books.json record. Printed folio = PDF p. − 2. Only the Matematica chapter (printed pp. 132–138) is placed here; every other chapter of the volume is another subject, and the chapter's process/attitude competence targets (MAT.I.7–9, MAT.II.9–11 and 13–16, MAT.III.9–20) carry no subject content and are stocked nowhere (MAT.III.14 restates the tool-use target placed once at item 330). CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the Piano states the competence targets pupils must reach and teaches nothing itself; its rows are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 5:** *MaMa — Linee guida del progetto MaMa: Ambito Numeri e calcolo* (teacher guide) — Switzerland, © MaMa – DECS – Divisione della scuola (CeRDD), 2021; 16,669,707 bytes, 102 PDF pp.; SHA-256 verified exact against its books.json record. Printed p. = PDF p. Read in full, cover to bibliography; §§1 and 3 (project history, platform, community) are apparatus, stocked nowhere, and §2's quoted Ticino Piano targets are placed at the Ticino items, not double-counted (record item R4). TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide directs teachers how to teach numbers and calculation; it is not a pupil textbook, and its rows are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 6:** *Lehrplan 21 Graubünden — Gesamtausgabe* (cantonal curriculum) — Switzerland, Erziehungs-, Kultur- und Umweltschutzdepartement Graubünden, Fassung vom 15.03.2016; German edition 60,184,274 bytes, 494 PDF pp.; SHA-256 verified exact against its books.json record. Printed folio = PDF p. − 2. ONE work in three filed language editions — German (read in full), Romansh *Plan d'instrucziun 21* (57,219,009 bytes, 488 PDF pp.) and Italian *Piano di studio 21 Grigioni* (56,644,563 bytes, 492 PDF pp.), both verified as the same work in translation (identical structure, pagination and MA competency codes) — and cited once. Only the Mathematik chapter (printed pp. 194–221) is placed here; every other subject in the volume is out of scope. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the plan states the competencies pupils must reach and teaches nothing itself; its rows are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure.

Switzerland enters this lesson as evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count — Switzerland has no pupil Maths textbook in hand; its in-hand set is three curriculum brochures, the mathematics chapters of two all-subjects curriculum volumes and one teacher guide, which state requirements and teaching guidance without being pupil textbooks (the standing evidence-only precedent). Switzerland's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-only): the whole in-hand set is read and stocked, and it advances only when a Swiss pupil Maths textbook — one of the prescribed commercial series, today licence-gated — lands in hand and is read.
